###Chapter 1: Chapter 1 Let's call off the wedding
Renea’s POV:
In the luxurious dressing room of the wedding shop, I stood before the mirror, the white lace of my wedding gown trailing behind me like a promise. My reflection looked back—skin as pale as the silk, eyes full of a mixture of hope and a strange, creeping unease.
"You look breathtaking, Miss Morris," the assistant whispered, her eyes full of genuine admiration.
"Thank you," I replied softly, taking a deep breath. Tomorrow was the day I would finally marry Jasper Thomas, the man I had loved for years. Yet, despite the elegance of the gown, my heart felt heavy, as if a storm were brewing just beyond the horizon.
Suddenly, the heavy velvet curtain was ripped open. Jasper stood there, his white suit a stark contrast to the cold, dark fury in his eyes. Before I could speak, he strode forward, his hand catching my shoulder with a force that sent a jolt of pain through my arm. He pressed me back against the cold wall, his face inches from mine.
"Renea Morris," he hissed, his voice trembling with a terrifying blend of rage and disgust. "Do you have any idea what you’ve done?"
I shook my head, my breath catching in my throat. I had never seen Jasper look at me with such absolute loathing.
"Still trying to play the innocent?" He laughed, a bitter, hollow sound. He pulled his phone from his pocket and thrust the screen in front of my face.
The screen flickered to life. My breath stopped. The video was clear—too clear. It showed a room I vaguely remembered from my bachelorette party, a night that remained a blur of alcohol and dizziness. On the screen, a woman who bore my face was caught in a compromising embrace with a man whose features were hidden, save for a distinct, wing-like birthmark on his shoulder blade.
My face turned ashen. The memories hit me like a physical blow—the party Kailey had organized, the drinks she had insisted I finish, the sudden heat that had consumed me, and waking up alone the next morning with a sense of dread that I had tried to bury.
"Jasper, listen to me..." I gasped, my hand trembling as I reached for his arm. "It’s not what it looks like. I was drugged... I don't even remember—"
"It doesn't matter what you remember!" Jasper interrupted, pulling away as if my touch were poisonous. "The video has already gone viral. The whole world knows what you are, Renea. I can't marry a woman who brings this kind of disgrace to my name."
The disdain from the shop assistants and the other patrons felt like shards of glass. Their whispers of "shameless" and "fraud" echoed in my ears, but all I could see was Jasper’s retreating back.
"Let’s call off the wedding," he said coldly. "I'm done."
He pushed me aside with such force that I stumbled, my head striking the edge of the mahogany dressing table. I felt the warm trickle of blood on my forehead, but the physical pain was nothing compared to the void opening in my chest.
I ignored the blood and the tears. I had to explain. I had to make him understand that I was a victim, not a betrayer. I gathered the heavy skirts of my gown and ran out of the shop, chasing the fading echo of his footsteps.
"I can't lose him," I whispered to myself, the white silk of my dress dragging through the dust of the street. "I have to find the truth."
###Chapter 2: Chapter 2 Sir, we are late
When Renea walked out of the bridal shop, she saw a white BMW parked outside the shop. Then a woman in a bright red dress got out of the car and greeted Jasper with a smile. 
The woman was none other than Kailey. 
Renea held up her heavy wedding dress and ran toward the car. She wanted to explain to Jasper that she didn’t cheat on him... 
However, Jasper didn’t even give her the chance to speak. Without even looking at Renea, who was running toward him, Jasper, opened the door of the BMW and sat down. Then he looked at Kailey who was standing outside, and urged her to get in. 
However, Kailey didn’t get in, but just looked at Jasper and smiled sweetly. Then, she looked at Renea who was running over in a mess, and said, “Jasper, you leave first. I have something to talk to Renea.” 
Jasper looked at her for a moment, then nodded. After that, he asked the driver to drive. Then his car disappears in the crowd. 
When Renea ran over, Jasper was already gone. Seeing this she immediately panicked. Renea held Kailey’s hand and said, “Kailey, Jasper had misunderstood me. You know what had happened that day... it was all a misunderstanding... Kailey, please you have to help me explain to Jasper... you know how much I love him... right...” 
Renea said, with a sobbing voice. Her hand that was holding Kailey’s arm was trembling. 
Kailey held Renea’s hand that was holding her arm and the gentle smile on her face was replaced by a cold one. 
She looked at Renea as if she was looking at a fool and said, “Renea, there is no need you to explain anything...” 
Renea was so nervous that she didn’t see the cold smile on her face. She looked in the direction where Jasper's car went and said, “No, I have to explain Jasper, I...” 
“What’s the point of explaining things to the person who has planned everything...” Kailey interrupted, as she shook Renea’s hand that was holding her arm away. 
Hearing her words, Renea paused. She looked at Kailey in confusion as if she didn’t understand the meaning behind her words, or you could say that she didn’t want to believe it... 
“Why did you look so shocked, Renea? Do you really think that Jasper loves you and wants to marry you...” Kailey said with a smirk. She continued, “Let me tell you, the whole video incident was planned by Jasper, so that he can get rid of us of the stupid marriage with you... he was the one who sent you in the bed of another man and spent a wonderful night with me...” 
“Renea, you are just a adopted daughter of the Morris family. Do you really think that Mom and Dad will let you inherit the Morris family...?” 
Every word that came out from Kailey’s mouth was like a sharp knife, which stabbed Renea’s heart, causing her to be badly injured. 
Kailey looked at Renea with her eyes filled with coldness and said, “Renea, why did you look so surprised. You are nothing but just a thief, a thief who stole my identity and everything belongs to me. I am the real daughter of the Morris family, but it was you who lived a life of a rich daughter. However, now you even want to steal the property that belongs to me...” 
“Ha! Dream on! I will never let you get anything that belongs to me... The family, the property, and even Jasper... it was all mine...” Kailey said coldly. Her whole face was filled with coldness and viciousness... 
After hearing everything Kailey said, Renea froze. Suddenly everything that happened in the past played in her head like a move and she realized that everything that happened in the past was all their schemes... 
Her adoptive parents purposefully showered her with indulgence, while her brother deliberately took her to numerous bars. And Kailey played her part by providing cover. 
They just wanted to trample on her dignity and pride at this moment today. 
However, Renea did not understand why. 
She could not figure out how she had let the Morris family down. 
Renea was adopted by Morris's family when she was very young. At that time, her adopted parents announced to the public that their real daughter had disappeared and to heal their wounds, they adopted a girl, Renea. 
When Renea came to the Morris family, she was very happy, because she thought she also had parents like other children. However, Renea didn’t receive any happiness or care from her adoptive parents. 
No matter how excellent her marks were or how many awards she received, her adoptive parents were always cold and indifferent toward her... 
In short, in the Morris family, except for her grandfather, no one had ever treated her well. 
However, not long after her grandfather's death, their adoptive parents suddenly said that they had found their real daughter and brought her back. 
Renea always felt guilty about Kailey that she was away from her parents for so long, so she did her best so that Kailey didn’t feel uncomfortable. She even concealed her brilliance, so that Kailey could shine and bask in the admiration of others. 
However, only now did she realize that she was a fool... 
It turned out that they did everything for the inheritance left by her grandfather. The love and care she gave them become nothing in front of the money...
Thinking all of this now, Renea felt ridiculous. 
Renea looked at Kailey coldly. Her eyes were burning with anger. She said, “Kailey Morris, I will never let you get successful in your plans. I will tell the whole world what kind of evil people the members of the Morris family are... I will let the whole world know your true colors...” 
After Renea finished speaking, she held her heavy wedding gown and was ready to leave. 
At this moment, Kailey looked at the car not standing far away and then taunted Renea who was walking in front of her, “Renea, you won’t get the chance... Because you are going to commit suicide because of guilt and shame...” 
Hearing her words, Renea stopped, as she didn’t know what was going on. She turned to look at Kailey, and saw a vicious smile on her face... 
“Tomorrow the whole city will know that the adopted daughter of the Morris family commits a suicide because of guilt and shame...” Kailey said with a smile... 
Before Renea could understand the meaning behind her words, Kailey walked over and pushed her toward the road. As soon as she pushed, Renea, a car sped toward Renea and hit her... 
Renea flew in the air because of the force and with a loud “bang” she was thrown to the ground. Renea was lying on the ground as the blood flowed from her head. Her white wedding dress turned red, because of the blood. Renea felt the unbearable pain in her body... 
She looked at Kailey, who was standing other side of the road and looking at her with a mocking smile, and her heart was filled with reluctance and remorse. 
“No... No... I can’t die like this... I have to live... I didn’t get my revenge yet... I can’t die like this...” Renea mumbled. Her eyes filled with reluctance... 
However, no matter how hard she tried, she was not able to do anything. Slowly her surroundings became quiet and everything around her turned black... 
**** 
Not far away, a black Roll-Royce stopped not far from the crowd. Then a man in a black suit gets out of the car. He strode toward Renea and checked her breathing. He furrowed his brows and his expression turned grave. 
At the very moment, a man donning a tailored suit also gets out of the car, exuding an aura of elegance and confidence. His striking features, ice gaze, and legal demeanor commanded attention. 
The man in a black suit said, “Sir, we are late.” 
Aron Kingston frowned and after a moment, he said lightly, “Give her a proper burial.
###Chapter 3: Chapter 3 Back for revenge
Boom! 
There was a loud bang as two cars collided. Then a bolt of lightning struck down from the sky, like splitting the dark sky with a flash of light. 
Because of the collision, the head of the girl sitting in the back seat of the car hit the front seat and she frowned in pain. 
Renea slowly opened her eyes and looked around. For a moment, she didn’t know what was going on... 
She remembered that Kailey had thrown her in front of the car, and she died. Then she looked around and found herself sitting in the car. 
Was I sitting in the car that took me to heaven? Then why do I still feel pain? She thought to herself. 
As Renea was still in confusion, her eyes fell on the woman sitting next to her in thin clothes...and as soon as she saw her, her eyes turned cold. It was Kailey. 
Seeing her sitting next to her, Renea's eyes were filled with anger. Everything she had done to her in the past, played in her head like a movie... Renea clenched her fists and was about to strangle her when the driver’s voice fell into her ear... 
“Miss Renea, are you alright? Someone just come in front of our car, and I have used the emergency break?” 
The driver’s words snapped her back into reality. Hearing his words, Renea paused, because she felt this scene was quite familiar... 
Then, Renea looked outside the window and was shocked by the scene in front of her. After all, she never forgot this road. This was the road she took seven years ago to bring back Kailey into the Morris family. 
Was I reborn? Did God give me another chance? Renea thought and pinched herself to check. As soon as she did, she felt a pain... 
After confirming that she was not dreaming and it was true, Renea turned her head to look at Kaliey and smiled faintly. However, her smile was cold and chilling.\ 
Even though she wanted to kill her, Renea knew that it was not the right time. Besides, she was not going to give her such an easy death... She will make sure that Kailey will live her life worse than death. 
Sensing the cold gaze on her body, Kailey raised her head and looked at Renea. Her eyes were radiating an air of purity and innocence that stirred pity in anyone who saw her. 
In her previous life, Renea was deceived by those innocent and pitiful eyes and felt guilty. She naively wanted to make up for it...
However, in this life, she already knows that behind those innocents she was hiding the heart of a snake, which was ready to bite her at any possible moment... 
“Renea, do you think Mom and Dad will like me?” Kailey asked nervously. 
Her hand between her legs clenched tighter. 
“Of course, they will like you. After all you are their real daughter.” Rene said with a smile. 
However, looking at Renea's smiling eyes, Kailey felt chill in her heart. She felt like Renea’s smile seemed friendly, yet it exuded a chilling aura. 
“Renea, I am not trying to steal Mom and Dad from you by following you back. I just want to know what my biological parents look like. I will be content as long as I can see them.” Kailey said and sobbed. She looked so pitiful that even the driver couldn’t help but sympathize with her. 
However, when Renea heard her words, she sneered as she looked at her fair and smooth hands. She lifted her hands and said, “What a delicate hand. It’s look like your foster parents have treated you very well all these years, right?” 
Kailey feels panic inside, could it be that Renea has found out the truth and knows that she's not really lost but forced to be out there like this, but for more than a decade her parents and brother have made her life privileged by visiting her every month. 
At first she didn't agree with what her parents were doing, but her grandfather ordered them to do it. 
Now that Grandpa is dead, she can finally return to the Morris family. 
Then Renea should die. But she quickly pulled herself together and answered, “Yes, they are nice to me.” 
In her previous life, Renea didn’t pay attention to it, feeling that Kailey’s foster parents’ kindness relieved some of her guilt. 
However, how could someone who lived in an impoverished and chaotic place like River Town, have such delicate hands? No matter how well Kailey's foster parents treated her, it was impossible for her not to do any household chores. 
However, such smooth hands could only be maintained by someone who had never done any household chores. 
Besides, Kailey was also proficient in piano, chess, dancing, and painting, which was not something the impoverished people in the poor town could afford. 
What’s more, for the last eighteen years, no one knows about the fact that the child was swapped. However, how come this truth came to light only a month after her grandfather’s death? 
Renea still remembers the day Susan brought her to donate blood. 
She was sure that it was all part of their scheme to expose her true identity. 
After all, how can a daughter who was lost for eighteen years be only found within two weeks of discovering Renea was not their biological daughter? 
Thinking of all this now, Renea's lips curled up into a mocking smile. It was look I was a fool in my past life. After all, even though the truth was always in front of my eyes, I chose not to see and close my eyes. 
Renea was not mocking Kailey but herself. For seven whole years, she didn’t notice their schemes and jumped into a fire pit dug by them for her. 
However, first, she likes to find out what had forced the Morris family into such a compromising situation.
###Chapter 4: Chapter 4 Give up on her room
At the Morris family house. 
As soon as the car arrived, Susan came out to welcome Kailey. 
“Oh, Kailey, why are you wearing such thin clothes? You might catch a cold,” Susan said in distress. 
Then she looked at Renea and reprimanded her fiercely, “How can you be so uncaring? Can’t you give your coat to your younger sister?” 
If it was in the past, Renea would certainly feel feisty. However, now... “I am cold too!” She said coldly. 
Susan was infuriated and was about to scold Renea when Kailey stopped her. 
“Mom, I am feeling cold.” 
“Sweetheart let’s go. You father is waiting for you.” Susan walked in with Kailey in her arms, completely ignoring Renea. 
Seeing their, back, Renea sneered. She had long been used to Susan’s indifference. 
Since Renea was little, Susan had never shown any concern for her. 
Despite her efforts, all she got was Susan’s impatient response. 
Even when she got first in her studies, Susan still scolded her, “What’s the use of studying so much?” 
In the past, Renea didn’t understand. She saw all parents praise their children when they did well in their studies so why Susan was so indifferent toward her? 
Only now did she realize how ridiculous she had been in her previous life. 
Renea was not Susan’s biological daughter. So, of course, she doesn’t care about Reena’s success or failure. 
However, it takes Renea's whole life to understand their schemes! 
Watching Renea following behind mother and daughter alone, the driver shook his head with pity. 
When Renea came inside, she saw various dishes on the table. However, none of them were Renea’s favorite. However, she didn’t find it strange. After all, her so-called parents never bothered to know what she liked or not. 
She was so used to it that she almost forgot what she liked to eat. 
Renea, hurry up and serve the food for us!” Susan commanded. 
“Mom, let me do it,” Kailey said as she was about to stand up. 
However, Susan stopped her and said, “How can that be. It was your first day at home. How can I let you do this.” 
Then she looked at Renea and said, “What are you daydreaming? Don’t you hear me?” 
Renea's lips curled up into a mocking smile. She sat down at the table and smiled as she spoke, “In the past, you could say that you were tough on me to help me grow up, but now, Kailey’s very first day here, you are treating me, your adopted daughter, so harshly. Aren’t you afraid of gossip and rumors?” 
“You...” Susan felt her blood pressure soar. 
Even after being raised for eighteen years, a wolf in sheep’s clothing remained wild, untamed, and unchanged. 
Renea used to be obedient. Was she beginning to reveal her true nature now? Susan thought to herself. 
Kailey grabbed Susan and said in a low voice, “Mom, don’t be angry at Renea because of me, or o won’t be at ease staying here.” 
“All right, all of you, sit down and eat.” Leon, who had been sitting silently in the main seat, spoke. 
Susan had no choice but to give up. She pulled Kailey to sit at the table and said, “Kailey, this is your father.” 
“Dad,” Kailey greeted timidly. 
Leon replied with a hum. He continued, “You must be hungry. Let’s eat.” 
Kailey nodded cautiously. 
She was obedient and sensible, winning the hearts of Susan and Leon. 
“Renea, clean up your room for Kailey after the meal.” Susan ordered in a deep voice. 
Susan said that without any prior discussion with Renea. It was as if it was a natural thing. 
Hearing this, Renea put down her fork and said coldly, “Don’t you understand what I said just now?” 
“What do you mean?” Susan gritted her teeth and glared at her. 
Had it not been for Sir Morris’s will, which transferred half of the Morris family’s inheritance to Renea, how could they have retained her within the Morris family? They would have expelled her a long time ago. 
Susan didn’t know what Sir Morris was thinking, having his two sons and grandson, yet giving the inheritance to an outsider. 
“Mistreating an adopted daughter may be considered a family matter on small scale, but on a large scale, it could tarnish the reputation of the Morris family.” 
Leon swallowed his words of rebuke and said coldly, “There are so many rooms in the house. Get Kailey another room.” 
Renea wasn’t surprised by his actions at all. Leon values the family’s reputation more than anything else. He naturally wouldn’t do anything that would damage the Morris family’s reputation. 
“I am full.” Renea stood up and smiled at Susan. 
The seemingly innocent smile, however, made Susan feel uneasy, and a cold chill ran down her spine.
###Chapter 5: Chapter 5 I have a breaking news
Susan felt that Renea’s smile had a sinister undertone. 
It was as if she could see through her. However, she quickly dissipated her doubts. Now that her biological daughter is finally home, Susan just wants to focus on her. 
Renea returned to her room. As she looked around the room, her lips curled up into a smirk. 
This was the only room in the house where no luxurious items could be seen. 
Even the clothes inside her wardrobe were not more than two hundred dollars. 
And the reason behind all this was because, Susan self-righteously stated, “To instill frugality in out children and prevent them from developing extravagant habits, we shall lead a frugal lifestyle.” 
However, to be honest, only Renea had been living a frugal life. 
On the other hand, Dylan was always dressed in designer clothes. 
With such a ridiculous reason, the Morris family gained a favorable impression and was praised for their good parenting skills. 
When Renea opened the wardrobe and looked at the collection of cheap clothing, her smile grew wilder. She took out her phone and placed an order from an online luxury brand store. 
The customer service representatives on duty looked at the order page and thought they were seeing things. 
After all, they could not imagine someone ordering more than ten sets of luxury clothing. 
Was it a prank? They thought. 
However, their surprise subsided when they saw that it was to be delivered to the Morris family in the West River District of Z city. 
Given the wealth of the Morris family, these few sets of clothing weren’t a big deal for them. 
However, wasn’t the Morris family always known for their frugal lifestyle? 
Why would they purchase so many luxury goods at once? 
Renea placed dozens of orders from every luxurious online retail store she could find online. 
Then, she dialed a contact number that she had deeply memorized. 
“Hello, who is it?” The man answered the phone impatiently. 
Renea said slowly, “I have some breaking news for you.” 
“What is it?” 
“It’s about the Morris family,” Renea emphasized each word. 
The man immediately became interested, “Tell me about it.” 
“I will send you the information and relevant evidence via text message. Just spread it according to my instructions. After that, I will give you the benefits you desire.” 
“Deal!” The man agreed. 
After Renea hung up the phone, a sneer appeared across her face. 
**** 
The next day. 
The loud sound of slamming the door woke Renea up from her dreams. 
As soon as Renea opened the door, Susan raised her hand and intended to slap her. Susan scolded, “Bitch, look at what you have done!” 
However, before the slap could land on Renea’s face, she grabbed Susan by the wrist and shook it off forcefully. 
Susan didn’t expect Renea would resist, so she was caught off guard and stumbled a few steps back. 
“Mom!” Kailey quickly supported Susan. 
The infuriated Susan berated even louder, “Renea, how dare you attack me!” 
Renea spoke softly, “I was just protecting myself.” 
The reply rendered Susan speechless and made her more furious, however, she could only question angrily, “Were you the culprit for the news on the internet? Did you buy the pile of clothes downstairs?” 
“What are you doing? Are you trying to ruin the Morris family’s reputation?” 
Early in the morning Leon called Susan and cursed. 
Susan didn’t even know what had happened. She only found out about the criticism of the Morris family when she turned on her phone. 
The reason for the criticism was that the Morris family, who had always claimed to be frugal, had purchased many luxury goods on the internet. 
“I heard they were all bought for their biological daughter.” 
“It’s apparent that they favor their biological daughter over the adopted one.” 
“How hypocritical! No wonders so many entertainers of the blue records have terminated their contracts. So, there is a reason.” 
The whole internet was filled with these types of comments. 
Renea chuckled and said, “Don’t you know that excess will lead to backlash?” 
“An ungrateful person will forever remain ungrateful, regardless of how they are raised!” Susan scolded Reena. Her voice was dripping with anger. 
Hearing Susan’s words Renea’s expression darkened. Then, she approached Susan with a hint of grimness in her eyes. 
“When have you ever raised me?” Renea asked with a cold smile.
###Chapter 6: Chapter 6 You never raised me
“From the moment I remember, you asked me to work with the servants. I am the one who has been doing all the housekeeping work in this house all these years. I even had to wash your feet.” Renea’s voice was cold as ice. 
When Renea was a child, she often wondered why her mother didn’t love her. 
“The servants get paid, but when did the Morris family ever given me a salary?” 
Susan was intimated by Renea’s cold and fierce momentum, and she subconsciously took a step back... 
For a fraction of a second, Susan saw the hatred in Renea’s eyes. It was like she wanted to kill her... 
However, it was only for a second and Susan felt that she was mistaken... 
“Dear mother, let me ask you a question. Have you really lost Kailey when she was a child? After all, from your actions and demeanor it didn’t seem like the case at all.” 
Hearing her words, Susan’s complexion turned unpleasant. Her gaze was evasive as she dared not meet Renea’s eyes. 
After all, Renea’s guess was right. 
Seeing the situation turn unfavorable toward them, Kailey immediately held onto Susan and spoke in a pitiful tone, “Mom, don’t be mad at Reena. If you act like this, I might think it’s all my fault.” 
“It’s just a few pieces of clothing. If Renea wants to buy them, then just let it be.” 
Kailey’s voice was sweet and soft! Renea had lived in this wealthy family for eighteen years, yet she couldn’t bring herself to say something so generous. 
However, Renea’s lack of reaction caused Susan’s frustration to grow even more. “We have spoiled you over so many years. You should learn some manners from your younger sister in the future.” Susan said angrily. 
Hearing Susan’s words, Renea burst into laughter. “How ridiculous!” she mumbled. 
“You!” Susan was so infuriated that her face turned green and white. 
“Miss Renea, you have a letter.” At this moment, the butler came up and handed over the letter. 
However, before Renea could receive it, Susan snatched it away and opened it. 
“An admission letter from the Dream High Academy?” Susan was shocked. 
After all, Susan had always thought that Renea was a simpleton, believing that even if Mr. Morris intended for Renea to join the Blue Records, it wouldn’t pose a threat to them. 
“How dare you apply for a media academy behind my back?” 
“Why do I need your permission? Who are you to me?” Renea replied indifferently. 
“Give this spot to Kailey. She excels in various arts and skills, so she is well-deserved to enter the Dream High Academy. It would be waste for you to go there,” Susan ordered. 
“Well-deserved? If you think she is so well-deserved, then why don’t you let her take the exam? It’s not like I have taken the last seat, and she didn’t have the chance, right?” After Renea finished speaking, she snatched the letter from Susan and intended to close the door. 
Susan had always boasted how proficient and excellent Kailey was in all arts and skills, yet she couldn’t even pass the admission exam of the Dream High Academy. 
Thinking about how Susan had been exceedingly attentive to her those days in the past so that she could relinquish her spot to Kailey... Renea’s lips curled up into a mocking smile. 
Renea blamed herself for being so naive in her past life. She thought that she could make them love her a little more by pleasing and compromising. 
She gave up the opportunity for Kailey and ended up attending an ordinary university. 
Kailey successfully debuted and became a glamorous and famous superstar... while she turned into an unwanted and detested paparazzo.
The more she couldn’t get it, the more she desired. 
Whenever Renea saw the family of four sitting on the living room sofa, laughing and chatting, she would feel envious. 
However, no matter how hard she tried she still couldn’t fit in them. 
All Renea gets in the end is betrayal and death. 
She was not foolish enough to make the same mistake again... 
Susan who stood outside, looked at the closed door and was fuming with anger. 
“Mom don’t be upset. It’s good thing that Renea manages to get into the Dream High Academy. When I get into in too, we can look after each other at school. If she ever needs help with anything, I will be there to assist her. It won’t let her tarnish the Morris family’s reputation.” Kailey consoled. 
Susan patted Kailey’s hand with gratitude and said, “Sweetie, you are the most sensible in our family.”
###Chapter 7: Chapter 7 Her life was not as significant as their benefits
As Kailey lowered her eyes slightly, a trace of coldness flashed across them. 
“Humph!” 
“I can’t believe that stupid Renea wants to enter the Dream High Academy. When the time comes, I will ensure everyone sees the difference between a real and a fake noble young lady!” 
“I want Rena to lose her reputation and get kicked out of the Morris family household.” Kailey contemplated. 
In the study. 
As soon as Susan entered the study, she began to complain, “Honey, you have no idea how arrogant Renea has become. Today trending topic on the internet was all her doing. I am telling she is intentionally trying to embarrass out family.” 
“What’s more she had even secretly taken an admission exam for the Dream High Academy without our knowledge!” 
Hearing this, Leon frowned slightly. 
“Honey, we should kick her out from the Morris family. After all, she is just an adopted daughter. Even if your father left her an inheritance, it is not hers.” 
Leon rebuked, “Bullshit! My day has made it clear that unless Renea has an accidental death, she will receive a share of the inheritance. We won’t get a single penny from her portion.” 
“In that case, let’s give her an accidental death,” Susan said nonchalantly. 
The way Susan was talking was like she was referring to a simple-minded matter. 
Although Renea stood outside the door with a calm expression, her hand holding the doorknob tightened.

“Mom, what we should now is not to get angry with dad about the things that had happened in the past. On the other hand, we should let him know that we are the one who are closet to him and care about him.” 
A sentence like this was a wake-up call for Susan. Which woke her up instantly, and made her think again about what she had just said and done. 
“I was really pissed off for a second. I will go to apologize your dad now!” Susan said said, as she was about to head towards the study. 
However, she was pulled back by Kailey, “Mom, I think it’s better if I go!” 
“Yes, yes, yes. It’s best if you go! Kailey, tell your father that I was just confused for a moment because of anger. And that I didn’t mean to be like that. I know that everything he was doing is for the sake of Morris family!” 
Now, Leon’s words and behavior were the same as Sir Morris’s back then. Forgiving Renea to the extreme. Although she didn’t know their intention, she knew one thing very well this was not a good start for her. 
If she alienated Leon from them because of her emotions, wouldn’t that mean that she was doing what Renea wanted? She didn’t want things like the inheritance to happen a second time. 
Now all they had to do was to let Leon know that they were the family. 
**** 
Kailey knocked on the door of the study and walked in. Leon’s face didn’t look any better when he saw her. 
“Dad, don’t be angry with mom. She said all that because she is being repressing things about my brother for too long.” 
At the mention of Dylan Leon’s face became even more ugly and he said, “Don’t mention Dylan in front of me again.” 
If it was not for Dylan, the company would not have been in such trouble again and several of the companies that had invested in it before had withdrawn their investments. 
Now that the play has started shooting and the money is not coming from anywhere, Leon can only mortgage to find a bank loan. 
However, it was a risky move for Blue Records Entertainment to mortgage for a drama. 
Whether a drama is good or not, will depend upon the time. 
Kailey stammered as she said, “Dad... there is something I don’t know if I should say or not!” 
“What?” Leon’s attitude was still unpleasant. 
“It’s about the brother. I hear that it was Renea who had provided evidence that the brother was the one who killed Miley.” 
Kailey said without hesitation, as she looked at Leon’s face capturing every single change in his expression. 
“Did your mom tell you that again?” Leon asked angrily. 
“No, dad please don’t blame mom. I went to see my brother at the police station after he was taken there!” 
Leon was about to get angry, but he was interrupted by Kailey’s words. 
“Dylan said that Renea went to him and admitted that she was responsible for what happened to my him and he also said that Renea did it all for the jade pendant.” 
Upon hearing the word “jade pendant” Leon’s face turns ugly. 
“Dad according to Dylan, as long as Renea get the jade pan dent she will let him go. However, if what brother said is true and sister has already gotten the jade pendant, then why she’s still refusing to let brother go?” 
Kailey said as she looked at Leon with a puzzled face. At this moment, Leon’s face was quite ugly and turned green and white from anger. 
“Is there something wrong with the jade pendant? Or is brother is lying? Dad, is this jade pendant involved in something beside sister’s birth?” 
Leon snapped back from his contemplation and angrily rebuked, “A jade pendant is a jade pendant. What can it be involved in? You don’t try to pray into what’s there?” 
“Yes.” 
Then Kailey said, “Dad, I not inquiring about anything. I just want our family to be well. Something happened to my brother, and now I’m the only child in the family, and it’s my responsibility to bear what should be borne.” 
Looking at Kailey’s aggravated appearance, Leon could not bear it any longer, and said, “Alright, you go out first. I want to be alone for a while.” 
“Yes.” Kailey retreated. 
Seeing Leon behaving like this, makes her even more convinced about the importance of the jade pendant. Kailey felt that since she could not inquire about the relationship Jade Pendant was involved in, it would be best if it never showed up in the world.
###Chapter 100: Chapter 100 Offical Announcement
“Hello Renea! Good News! I have good news for you. Guess what happened! Forget it, let me tell you, the actress that I have been eyeing, Amelia, who works for K.W. Media has come to the audition today!” Carson said excitedly. 
Compared to before when Carson found the heroine of his heart, he is even more excited today. 
“Yeah, congratulations!” Renea said flatly, as if she had already guessed it. 
“Since all the actors are currently finalized, we can start the movie when the New Year is over,” Carson said to himself. 
Renea listens quietly.

“Hmm.”

“Happy New Year.” 
Renea hung up the phone and looked out the window. It was only 29 today, and the fireworks were already thundering outside. 
The window view outside is as lively as it’s online right now.

[@Hello Renea, Aria Adair]

Does anyone remember, the video of Renea on the internet a while ago in which she has been misunderstood as the Sugar baby? At that time, Kevin had personally tweeted to clarify for Renea. When did Kevin personally send out a tweet to clarify a scandal for someone?”

The online public opinion is getting more and more outrageous and most of the people were scolding Renea. 
After all, in the eyes of the book fans, if Renea played the Aria, it was like ruining an original plot. 
When Renea went to check on her Twitter account, she found that her private message box had already exploded. But she didn’t even click it. After re-tweeting Kevin’s tweet, she went offline. 
That’s the reason why Amelia auditioned for it. 
Of course, she was not going to make a deal with Kevin because she wanted Amelia. 
The script was so intriguing that it made her feel like she was right there in the middle of it and that’s made her decide to take on the show as a way to lay the groundwork for a fortunate-behind-the-scene movie. 
Besides Kevin is the best director, and she could learn a lot of things from him. 
****

The word sister was like a taboo in the White family. 
A while later... 
The cold light on Kevin’s face disappeared, and he said, nonchalantly, “You are the one with the nasty thoughts. I chose her because I think she’s suitable for the role of Aria.”

After Richard became famous by playing a role in an immortal drama, he has not worked in any immortal since then.

As an actor, he wants to be diversified, so that he can give the audience the feeling that they will not only remember him because of his one role, but he wants that each role can give the audience a different view.

To be honest, Kevin was planning to do just that. It was just that Richard’s rebellious tamper could only go against the grain.
